Roblox Hitbox Detection Research Handoff

Goal



Create an executor-side Lua script that:



Detects when the local player performs animation:

rbxassetid://10479335397

Detects when that attack actually hits another player/entity.

Only triggers when:

The local player is the attacker.

The victim is confirmed by the game's own hitbox system.

The victim's torso/body is the hit target.

Victim is within 30 studs.

When confirmed, play these assets once:

rbxassetid://102896911709401

rbxassetid://127621297686566

rbxassetid://121170635692427



Environment:



Roblox executor: Potassium

Executor scripts, not Roblox Studio LocalScripts.

Important Findings



The game does NOT rely on simple .Touched detection.



It has a custom event system:



ReplicatedStorage

 └── EventTypes

      ├── Hitboxes

      └── Movement



Movement modules receive already-calculated victims.



Confirmed Movement Module Data

EventTypes.Movement.Knockback



The module receives:



onLivePlay(u32, p33, p34)



Where:



u32 = attacker character

p34 = hit data



The hit data contains:



p34.PrimaryVictim

p34.Victims



Example:



if p34.PrimaryVictim and p34.PrimaryVictim.Parent then

    applyTo(p34.PrimaryVictim)

end



and:



if p34.Victims then

    for i in pairs(p34.Victims) do

        applyTo(i)

    end

end



Meaning the game already knows exactly who was hit.



EventTypes.Movement.Launch



Confirmed the same system:



function v9.onLivePlay(p15,p16,p17)





Where:



p15 = attacker

p17 = victim data



Uses:



p17.PrimaryVictim



or:



p17.Victims



Example:



fireLaunch(p15, p17.PrimaryVictim, v18)



Therefore the hitbox modules are responsible for creating p17.



Current Theory



The correct hook point is NOT:



GetPartBoundsInBox

GetTouchingParts

torso collision checking



Those are inaccurate.



The correct method:



Find the Hitbox module that creates:



{

    PrimaryVictim = victim,

    Victims = {

        [victim] = true

    }

}



Then hook its onLivePlay.



Expected final logic:



if attacker == LocalPlayer.Character then



    if currentAnimation == "10479335397" then



        for victim in pairs(data.Victims) do



            if victim torso exists

            and distance <= 30 then



                playAssets()



            end



        end



    end



end

Files Already Checked

Checked:

ReplicatedStorage.EventTypes.Movement.Knockback

ReplicatedStorage.EventTypes.Movement.Launch



These only consume victim data.



Files Needed Next



Please analyze:



Highest priority:

ReplicatedStorage.EventTypes.Hitboxes.Standard



This is likely the default melee hitbox.



Also useful:



ReplicatedStorage.EventTypes.Hitboxes.Radius

ReplicatedStorage.EventTypes.Hitboxes.ClosestToMouse

What To Look For In Standard



Find code involving:



Victims

PrimaryVictim

HumanoidRootPart

GetPartBoundsInBox

GetPartBoundsInRadius

OverlapParams

Raycast

Hitbox



Especially functions like:



onLivePlay()



or:



CreateHitbox()

Continue Here

local function _getBranchProjectilePreviewCFForDiag(p150, p151) -- Line: 399

    -- upvalues: u5 (ref)

    local v152 = u5(p150);



    if v152 == "" then

        return nil, "empty_branch";

    end;



    local v153 = rawget(_G, "MoveEditorBranchProjectileCFByName");



    if type(v153) ~= "table" then

        return nil, "missing_store";

    end;



    local v154 = v153[v152];

    local v155 = false;



    if v154 == nil then

        local v156 = string.lower(v152);



        for i, v in pairs(v153) do

            if type(i) == "string" and string.lower(u5(i)) == v156 then

                v154 = v;

                v155 = true;

                break;

            end;

        end;

    end;



    if typeof(v154) == "CFrame" then

        if v155 then

            return v154, "legacy_direct_casefold";

        end;



        return v154, "legacy_direct";

    end;



    if type(v154) ~= "table" then

        local v157 = nil;



        if v155 then

            return v157, "missing_branch_store_casefold";

        end;



        return v157, "missing_branch_store";

    end;



    local v158 = tonumber(p151);



    if v158 then

        local v159 = v154[v158];



        if typeof(v159) == "CFrame" then

            return v159, "owner_match";

        end;

    end;



    local _last = v154._last;



    if typeof(_last) == "CFrame" then

        return _last, "last_fallback";

    end;



    for _, v in pairs(v154) do

        if typeof(v) == "CFrame" then

            return v, "first_any_fallback";

        end;

    end;



    return nil, "no_cframe_in_branch_store";

end;

local function _extractClientLiveCFrame(p254) -- Line: 570

    if typeof(p254) == "CFrame" then

        return p254, "direct";

    end;



    if type(p254) == "table" then

        for _, v in ipairs({

            p254.CFrame,

            p254.cframe,

            p254.CF,

            p254.cf,

            p254.Pivot,

            p254.pivot,

            p254.RootCFrame,

            p254.rootCFrame

        }) do

            if typeof(v) == "CFrame" then

                return v, "table";

            end;

        end;



        return nil, "table_no_cframe";

    end;



    if p254 == nil then

        return nil, "nil";

    end;



    return nil, "unsupported";

end;
